# Rust exit parity ledger: owner summary

Generated by `node Tools/agent/rust-parity-ledger.mjs`; do not edit. `--check` regenerates it.

Rust tree: `020d8527e50255391fcc3613fdc5736fb9c26732`. Jet tree: `ab64c8e1434e25bbd89bb77f3bf1cecc00f6846f`. Secondary Jet trees: `origin/port/main` (`61693bcbd7cc72272b6988cc3e38ebaf9efe7bf9`), `cutover-ready` (`98a9c40469b217f17b400fe5065426aec41abec0`).

Rows: `items.tsv` (one per Rust fn, method, type, trait, macro), `tests.tsv` (one per Rust `#[test]`), `cli.tsv` (Jet CLI refusals), `offdev.tsv` (Jet definitions that exist only on a secondary tree). Statuses:

- `ported`: a Jet item cites the Rust item (`port-comment`: `// Rust: path:line`; `rust-name-comment`: a comment naming Rust `name`), carries its exact or prefixed name (`name-exact`, `name-prefixed`), is a method of the same type (`jet-method`), is `<rest>` in the file defining the Jet type spelled by the name's leading words (`type-prefixed`), or is prefixed by a word of the Rust file name (`stem-prefixed`).
- `ported-differs`: `file-level` (a Jet file cites the Rust file, but no Jet item matches this item by name or line) or `jet-string-table` (a Jet string literal names the item, as a generated or registry table row, but no Jet definition does).
- `kept`: the Rust file survives the cutover at a new path (FFI bridge runtime and support crates).
- `not-ported`: no Jet counterpart on the Jet tree; `only-on:<ref>` means the port exists only on that secondary tree.
- `dropped`: retired with Rust: `rust-emit-backend` (the Rust-emitting backend and its runtime prelude), `rust-only-glue` (build scripts, the Rust bootstrap host, stage0 repack, std trait impls), `rust-test-harness` (non-test helpers of the Rust integration tests), `vendored-upstream`.
- tests: `mapped` (`mapping-tsv`; `jet-test-name`: same #Test description or test fn; `port-comment`: a Jet comment cites a line of the test; `testkit-case`; `ui-fixture`: `tests/ui/<name>.*`; `fixture`; `jet-test-suffix`: a Jet test name ends with the Rust name once separators are dropped; `jet-test-words`: a Jet test name holds every Rust word in order plus at most 40% more; `jet-test-overlap`: at least 4 shared words, 80% of the Rust words and half of the Jet words), `retired` (listed in `Tools/testkit/retired-with-rust.tsv`), `dropped` (tests of the Rust-emit backend), `not-mapped` (`case-dir:` names the testkit directory of the same Rust file).

## Triage of unmatched rows

Every `not-ported` item and `not-mapped` test carries a reviewed class from `triage.tsv` (columns `class`, `group`, `class_evidence` of `items.tsv` and `tests.tsv`): `covered` (a Jet counterpart exists; the evidence cites its `path:line`), `missing` (Jet lacks the behavior; `partial:` names what Jet has and lacks, `untested:` a test whose behavior Jet has without a Jet test), `obsolete` (no meaning in Jet: Rust-host plumbing, a retired surface, or a Jet design that makes it unnecessary; cited). Group rationales live in `triage-groups.tsv`. `rust lines` sums the Rust source lines of the rows. The ledger checks every cited Jet `path:line` exists and that a cited definition name sits within three lines of it; `--check` fails on any unclassified row or triage problem.

## Missing behavior (owner list)

Groups holding Rust items Jet lacks, largest first; each names what is absent. 400 groups.

| group | items | tests | rust lines | rationale |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `jetos-system-management` | 266 | 17 | 10726 | JetOS system management (os, user, push verbs: generations, activation, NixOS import and backend, installer media, VMs, initrd, desktop store). The Jetpack port explicitly leaves the JetOS verbs out and JetOS is a later epoch: deferred, not retired. |
| `web-emitter-unmatched` | 120 | 0 | 3072 | absent: no same-stem Jet Web emitter function found for the remaining MIRWeb.rs functions (route, release, descriptor, json and other js_* helpers) |
| `prove-pipeline` | 110 | 4 | 4569 | absent: jet prove pipeline (run_prove, child supervision, .jetproof writer, claim grades, render_report, replay save/index), refused by Compiler/JetCli/Source/Cli/Front.jet:1543 |
| `budget-providers` | 73 | 13 | 3184 | absent: budget provider and measurement subsystem (providers, wait4 child timing, compile probes, isolated workers, file transport) |
| `jetpack-studio` | 79 | 7 | 2843 | Jetpack studio server and transactions (the studio verb); outside the Jetpack port with the JetOS verbs. |
| `progress-multi` | 77 | 8 | 860 | absent on dev; off-dev port at origin/port/main:Compiler/JetCli/Source/Cli/Output/Progress.jet |
| `publish-foreign-package` | 80 | 0 | 2055 | absent on dev; off-dev port at origin/port/main:Jetpack/Publication/Source/ForeignPackage.jet:16 |
| `import-translators` | 78 | 0 | 2729 | absent: `jet import` source translators (foreign header/JavaScript/Pascal/Ada/enterprise translation, import plan, evidence receipts, update acceptance); registry declares `import` at Compiler/JetFoundation/Source/Registry/CLICommands.jet:404 with no implementation |
| `prove-solver` | 55 | 13 | 2056 | absent on dev; off-dev port at origin/port/main:Compiler/JetCli/Source/Cli/Evidence/Solver.jet:2 |
| `budget-command` | 64 | 2 | 2418 | absent: `jet budget` command (build gates, stored baselines, measurement report, emit/check) |

## Matcher residual (hand-checked samples)

Random rows hand-checked against the Jet tree; every row with its exact Jet location or the searches run is in `samples/`. `now` counts the sampled rows the current ledger still classifies the sampled way (still unmatched, or still matched by the same evidence); verdicts and rates are over those rows. For unmatched rows, `found` is a matcher false negative (a Jet counterpart exists) and `partial` is related Jet code covering part of the job. For loosely matched rows, `wrong` is a matcher false positive.

| round | kind | matcher | pool | rows | now | verdicts | rate | evidence |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| r1 | items | 7aff95cf5 ledger | not-ported none | 60 | 51 | absent 37, found 9, obsolete 5 | found 18%, found+partial 18% | `samples/r1-items.tsv` |
| r1 | tests | 7aff95cf5 ledger | not-mapped | 40 | 30 | absent 22, found 6, obsolete 2 | found 20%, found+partial 20% | `samples/r1-tests.tsv` |
| r2 | items | method/comment/stem rules, before Jet string lexing | not-ported none | 60 | 55 | absent 35, found 5, obsolete 3, partial 12 | found 9%, found+partial 31% | `samples/r2-items.tsv` |
| r2 | tests | ui-fixture/test-words rules, before test-suffix and test port comments | not-mapped, not only-on | 40 | 38 | absent 22, found 1, obsolete 1, partial 14 | found 3%, found+partial 39% | `samples/r2-tests.tsv` |
| r3 | items | final rules (pool also held name-words matches, since removed) | not-ported none | 60 | 60 | absent 24, found 5, obsolete 12, partial 19 | found 8%, found+partial 40% | `samples/r3-items.tsv` |
| r3 | tests | final rules (pool before the port-comment start-line rule) | not-mapped, not only-on | 40 | 40 | absent 21, found 1, obsolete 5, partial 13 | found 3%, found+partial 35% | `samples/r3-tests.tsv` |
| r3 | fp-items | loose item rules as sampled (name-words since removed; jet-string-table since ported-differs) | ported by loose rules | 20 | 9 | correct 6, related 2, wrong 1 | wrong 11%, related 22% | `samples/r3-fp-items.tsv` |
| r3 | fp-tests | loose test rules as sampled (header-range port comments since excluded) | mapped by loose rules | 20 | 18 | correct 16, related 1, wrong 1 | wrong 6%, related 6% | `samples/r3-fp-tests.tsv` |
